## 3. Scope and constraints — nonnegotiable

1. **Coverage:** Full **Peninsular Malaysia FIRST**. Kuala Lumpur is an initial pilot, not final coverage. Sabah and Sarawak only after Peninsular works well.
2. **Protect original Mterms2026 app/files, health and data.** Do not disrupt unrelated oilandgas/other services using the shared app. User previously allowed brief Heroku downtime during necessary deploys but prefers safe, targeted changes.
3. Keep unverified changes on isolated dev branches/draft PRs; **no automatic merge or deployment** of unverified experimental routing. Do not expose credentials. No MongoDB writes except deliberately planned, isolated, approved service operation.
4. Reuse existing code and verified CI results. **Avoid test-for-test's-sake, redundant PRs and repetitious rework**. Prioritize usable app integration, route accuracy and real-world acceptance. Run targeted tests for changes and only relevant full suites.
5. Work autonomously within these safety bounds; user does not want repeated 'go' requests. Ask only for genuinely required external access or decisions.
6. Never claim offline test success establishes real-world turn legality or deployed production behavior.
7. This file is the **handover source of truth for project coordination**, but verify time-sensitive status against live GitHub/Heroku evidence. Update after each meaningful change/verification, even if no code changed.

Update this document with `fetch_file` + `update_file` using current blob SHA; never overwrite concurrent changes or edit unrelated code.
5. For solely documentation updates, main is acceptable; for code, use isolated branch/draft PR unless a verified, explicitly suitable deployment process is established.

### 2026-11-18 11:29 MYT — PR72: distinguish GPS drift from plausible road departures

- Owner required road/motion logic: a moving car cannot instantly jump sideways, while deliberate/accidental turns should be plausible for its speed and an available road. Existing published PR60 correctly preserved measured position but displayed it directly, exposing ordinary drift outside the blue route. Implemented a separate display/motion matcher without altering stored measured GPS.
- Route-aligned display: small offsets use selected-route geometry (12–24 m bounded corridor scaled by reported accuracy); measured `gpsPosition` remains independent from `vehiclePosition`. Pending drift/turn observations cannot advance the stateful route predictor, progress or provider origin. The triangle stays visible; uncertain alignment uses existing grey treatment. Camera/Recenter follows the display position while retaining existing measured-fix trust checks.
- Departure evidence: coherent movement across readings, heading versus travel direction, and a conservative per-reading speed × heading-change / time screen (5 m/s² heuristic with one second sample-lag allowance). Missing GPS speed derives movement speed rather than bypassing the screen. Gradual exits can accumulate a turn; abrupt high-speed right-angle changes are held. A supported new road needs at least three samples over two seconds. Established departure geometry is retained near the old route instead of snapping back between parallel corridors.
- Nearby-road support reuses loaded MapLibre transportation features only: bounded projected query, car-road classes, deduplicated geometries, max 80 lines, local segment projection and nearby geometric junction check. Bridge/tunnel/layer ambiguity and elevated baseline crossings do not prove connectivity. No new API, key, paid map service or network request. **Rendered road geometry/kinematic thresholds are heuristics, not authoritative lane, legal-turn or grade connectivity proof.** Where geometry is unavailable, four coherent readings over at least eight seconds can allow route recovery but the display remains unverified/grey. Loaded evidence with no supported connection is not treated as proof of a turn.
- Runtime: `predictive-gps.js` adds `createRoadMotion`; controller publishes display position/certainty/mode separately and gates deviation/reroute verdicts; Passenger UI uses that display position for marker/camera; native adapter exposes bounded nearby road geometry. Existing accepted-raw GPS jump/age/order guards, continuous marker/watch and atomic route replacement retained. Cache versions predictive 4-5, controller 4-12, UI 28, vector 7. Eleven frontend/test/workflow paths.
- Validation: **53/53** local provider/controller/motion tests PASS, syntax and diff checks PASS. Ten focused motion cases cover small drift, single lateral jump/recovery, slow connected turn/stopping, abrupt highway turn, missing speed, gradual exit, parallel/elevated candidates, absent connection, elevated baseline and unavailable tiles. Real-controller regression verifies pending drift does not feed route history or trigger requests. Added no-network 390px browser case verifies snapped display with retained measured coordinates, low-speed connected fixture turn, one reroute request and identical triangle during/after route swap; existing UI/provider/GPS regressions preserved. Early local checks exposed a corridor expectation and retry-after-stop reset, both corrected; all final results refer to fixed source.
- Final reviewed head `98c034645a4f6ba15e64e635abc45683ca96122b`: [JALAN public KL experimental privacy guard](https://github.com/Zux12/jalan-navigation/actions/runs/44230034243), [JALAN TomTom route revocation and fallback safety](https://github.com/Zux12/jalan-navigation/actions/runs/44230034249), [JALAN public non-phone Chromium 390px integration gate](https://github.com/Zux12/jalan-navigation/actions/runs/44230034231) all terminal SUCCESS.
